[user] Memorize and track the chain(s) of variable assignment hidden in the following text.

[The relevant text is in a separate document accessible via the read_chunk tool — see the system prompt for usage.] (Document length: 3997 tokens.)
Question: Find all variables that are assigned the value 98715 in the text above.

Reply with the answer inside \boxed{}.
[assistant] This document is 3997 tokens — too long to read in one context. The result over a range does not depend on the order in which the document is read, so disjoint ranges can be computed independently and merged. The question asks to find all variables assigned the value 98715, so the state is the set of variable=value facts seen so far. I therefore split the range in half recursively, having a subagent collect every variable=98715 assignment (collecting every variable=value fact) over each half and combining the two partials. Once I have it for the whole document, I turn the combined result into the final answer.
Range 0..3997 is not below 500; splitting at midpoint 1998.
